2. Key Technical Concepts:

   - MP3 audio output (also flac/wav/pcm available)

     ```js
     const QUEUE_BASE = "https://console.gmicloud.ai/api/v1/ie/requestqueue/apikey/requests";
     const LLM_BASE = "https://api.gmi-serving.com/v1/chat/completions";
     // TTS payload: { text, voice_id, speed, vol, pitch, emotion, language_boost, format, audio_sample_rate, bitrate, channel, sound_effects }
     // Music payload: { lyrics, prompt, sample_rate, bitrate, format }
     ```
   - `scripts/gen-assets.mjs` - Created. Bulk asset generator that reads `data/asset-spec.json`, iterates jobs (kind: "tts" | "music" | "sfx-tts"), writes MP3s to `src/assets/audio/{speech,music,sfx}/`, and updates `manifest.json`. Supports `--only music` / `--only speech-en` etc. Resumable - skips already-done items.

     ```js
     const RENDER_TARGET_WIDTH = 640;
     const RENDER_TARGET_HEIGHT = 360;
     // Two-pass render: scene -> renderTarget -> canvas via full-screen quad
     // renderTarget uses NearestFilter, sRGBColorSpace, color crush in fragment shader
     ```

   - `src/ui/dialogue.ts` - Dialogue UI controller. 155 lines.
   - `src/ui/hud.ts` - HUD with cash, day, time, prompt, toast.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - three.js 0.169 WebGLRenderer with `antialias: true` (changed from false)
   - `preserveDrawingBuffer: true` for Playwright screenshot support
   - Internal 480x270 canvas buffer with CSS `image-rendering: pixelated` for pixel-art aesthetic
   - Mouse-look with TWO code paths: pointer-locked (uses `movementX/Y`) vs hover (uses `clientX/Y` deltas)
   - Browser quirk: `e.movementX/Y` is 0 when not pointer-locked — must compute deltas manually
   - Pointer Lock API failures in headless environments (try/catch required)
   - Keyboard event handling on both `window` and `canvas` with `preventDefault()` on movement keys
   - Playwright readPixels for direct WebGL pixel sampling (bypasses screenshot rendering issues)
   - `window.__aitrainer` debug hook pattern for verifying game state from outside

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`/home/lucas/DEV/Projects/ai-trainer-simulator/src/engine/renderer.ts`** (MODIFIED)
     - Why important: This is the core three.js renderer. The antialias setting was making edges look horrible per user feedback.
     - Changed: `antialias: false` → `antialias: true` with explanatory comment that pixel-art look comes from CSS upscale, not from disabling MSAA
     ```ts
     const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({
       canvas,
       // Antialias ON. The 480x270 buffer is still tiny so the GPU MSAA cost
       // is negligible, and the jagged edges look horrible at any zoom. The
       // CSS `image-rendering: pixelated` upscale on the canvas element is
       // what gives us the pixel-art look, not per-fragment disabling of MSAA.
       antialias: true,
       powerPreference: "high-performance",
       preserveDrawingBuffer: true,
     });
     ```

   - **`/home/lucas/DEV/Projects/ai-trainer-simulator/src/engine/controls.ts`** (MODIFIED)
     - Why important: User's main complaint was broken controls — only one mouse axis worked, WASD did nothing, clicking broke everything.
     - Key fix: Added `lastClient` tracker to compute mouse deltas from `clientX/Y` when not pointer-locked (since `movementX/Y` are 0 in that state)
     - Added try/catch around requestPointerLock (it throws in headless)
     - Added keyboard handlers on BOTH canvas and window with preventDefault
     ```ts
     // New: lastClient tracker
     let lastClient = { x: 0, y: 0, valid: false };
     
     // Try/catch around requestPointerLock
     canvas.addEventListener("click", () => {
       if (!pointerLocked) {
         try {
           canvas.requestPointerLock?.();
         } catch {
           // Fall back to hover-to-look
         }
       }
     });
     
     // Dual-path mouse-look
     document.addEventListener("mousemove", (e) => {
       if (!(pointerLocked || e.target === canvas)) return;
       if (pointerLocked) {
         mouseDelta.x += e.movementX;
         mouseDelta.y += e.movementY;
       } else {
         if (!lastClient.valid) {
           lastClient.x = e.clientX;
           lastClient.y = e.clientY;
           lastClient.valid = true;
         } else {
           mouseDelta.x += e.clientX - lastClient.x;
           mouseDelta.y += e.clientY - lastClient.y;
           lastClient.x = e.clientX;
           lastClient.y = e.clientY;
         }
       }
     });
     canvas.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => { lastClient.valid = false; });
     
     // Keyboard on both canvas and window with preventDefault
     const moveKeys = new Set(["w", "a", "s", "d", "arrowup", "arrowdown", "arrowleft", "arrowright", "shift"]);
     canvas.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
       const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
       keys.add(k);
       if (moveKeys.has(k)) e.preventDefault();
     });
     canvas.addEventListener("keyup", (e) => {
       keys.delete(e.key.toLowerCase());
     });
     window.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
       const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
       keys.add(k);
       if (moveKeys.has(k)) e.preventDefault();
     });
     window.addEventListener("keyup", (e) => {
       keys.delete(e.key.toLowerCase());
     });
     ```

   - **`/home/lucas/DEV/Projects/ai-trainer-simulator/src/main.ts`** (MODIFIED)
     - Why important: Added debug hooks to enable Playwright verification of player/camera state without source modifications
     - Added at the end, after "// --- Boot ---" comment:
     ```ts
     declare global {
       interface Window {
         __aitrainer?: {
           getPlayer: () => { x: number; y: number; z: number };
           getCamera: () => { x: number; y: number; z: number };
           getKeys: () => string[];
           isLocked: () => boolean;
         };
       }
     }
     window.__aitrainer = {
       getPlayer: () => {
         const p = controls?.getPlayerPosition();
         return p ? { x: p.x, y: p.y, z: p.z } : { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 };
       },
       getCamera: () => {
         if (!engine) return { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 };
         return { x: engine.camera.position.x, y: engine.camera.position.y, z: engine.camera.position.z };
       },
       getKeys: () => Array.from(keys),
       isLocked: () => controls?.isPointerLocked() ?? false,
     };
     ```

         focusedNpcId = id;
         if (!cameraDirector || !engine || !sceneObjects) return;
         if (id === null) {
           cameraDirector.snapTo(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.5, 6), new THREE.Vector3(0, 4.5, 9));
           return;
         }
         const npc = NPCS.find((n) => n.id === id);
         if (!npc) return;
         const target = new THREE.Vector3(npc.position.x, npc.position.y + 0.6, npc.position.z);
         const offset = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1.6, 3.2);
         cameraDirector.panTo(target, offset);
       }

       ```ts
       function raycastNpc(clientX: number, clientY: number): NPC | undefined {
         if (!engine || !sceneObjects) return undefined;
         const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
         const ndc = new THREE.Vector2(
           ((clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1,
           -((clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1,
         );
         const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
         raycaster.setFromCamera(ndc, engine.camera);
         const targets: THREE.Object3D[] = [];
         for (const mesh of sceneObjects.npcMeshes.values()) targets.push(mesh);
         const hits = raycaster.intersectObjects(targets, true);
         for (const hit of hits) {
           let obj: THREE.Object3D | null = hit.object;
           while (obj) {
             const id = (obj.userData as { npcId?: string }).npcId;
             if (id) {
               const npc = NPCS.find((n) => n.id === id);
               if (npc) return npc;
             }
             obj = obj.parent;
           }
         }
         return undefined;
       }
       ```
     - Added mousedown listener on canvas for click-to-talk (only left button, no shift)
     - In frame loop: calls `controls.update(dt)` when not in dialogue, updates `playerGroup.position` and `playerGroup.rotation.y = -controls.getYaw()`

   - `/home/lucas/DEV/Projects/ai-trainer-simulator/src/engine/collision.ts` (NEW)
     - Pure AABB collision function: `applyWithCollision(pos, radius, deltaX, deltaZ, bounds, obstacles) -> XZ`
     - Critical implementation: uses `outX`/`outZ` accumulators so x-axis success persists when z-axis is also processed:
       ```ts
       export function applyWithCollision(pos, radius, deltaX, deltaZ, bounds, obstacles): XZ {
         let outX = pos.x;
         let outZ = pos.z;
         if (deltaX !== 0) {
           let x = pos.x + deltaX;
           if (x - radius < bounds.minX) x = bounds.minX + radius;
           if (x + radius > bounds.maxX) x = bounds.maxX - radius;
           if (!overlapsAny(x, pos.z, radius, obstacles)) outX = x;
         }
         if (deltaZ !== 0) {
           let z = pos.z + deltaZ;
           if (z - radius < bounds.minZ) z = bounds.minZ + radius;
           if (z + radius > bounds.maxZ) z = bounds.maxZ - radius;
           if (!overlapsAny(outX, z, radius, obstacles)) outZ = z;
         }
         return { x: outX, z: outZ };
       }
       ```
